# Interactive Prototyping: The Clock of Pi
Shifeng Hong sh2769

Does it feel like time is moving strangely during this semester?

For our first Pi project, we will pay homage to the [timekeeping devices of old](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/History_of_timekeeping_devices) by making simple clocks.

It is worth spending a little time thinking about how you mark time, and what would be useful in a clock of your own design.

**Please indicate anyone you collaborated with on this Lab here.**
Be generous in acknowledging their contributions! And also recognizing any other influences (e.g. from YouTube, Github, Twitter) that informed your design.

## Part A. 
### Connect to your Pi
Just like you did in the lab prep, ssh on to your pi. Once you get there, create a Python environment (named venv) by typing the following commands.

```
ssh pi@<your Pi's IP address>
...
pi@raspberrypi:~ $ python -m venv venv
pi@raspberrypi:~ $ source venv/bin/activate
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~ $ 

```
### Setup Personal Access Tokens on GitHub
Set your git name and email so that commits appear under your name.
```
git config --global user.name "Your Name"
git config --global user.email "yourNetID@cornell.edu"
```

The support for password authentication of GitHub was removed on August 13, 2021. That is, in order to link and sync your own lab-hub repo with your Pi, you will have to set up a "Personal Access Tokens" to act as the password for your GitHub account on your Pi when using git command, such as `git clone` and `git push`.

Following the steps listed [here](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens) from GitHub to set up a token. Depends on your preference, you can set up and select the scopes, or permissions, you would like to grant the token. This token will act as your GitHub password later when you use the terminal on your Pi to sync files with your lab-hub repo.


## Part B. 
### Try out the Command Line Clock
Clone your own lab-hub repo for this assignment to your Pi and change the directory to Lab 2 folder (remember to replace the following command line with your own GitHub ID):

```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~$ git clone https://github.com/<YOURGITID>/Interactive-Lab-Hub.git
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~$ cd Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab\ 2/
```
Depends on the setting, you might be asked to provide your GitHub user name and password. Remember to use the "Personal Access Tokens" you just set up as the password instead of your account one!

Check if the directory has clone sucessfully, you should see the Interactive-Lab-Hub under the home directory listed:
```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~ $ ls
Bookshelf      Documents            Music     Public                 venv
create_img.sh  Downloads            pi-apps   screen_boot_script.py  Videos
Desktop        Interactive-Lab-Hub  Pictures  Templates
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~ $
```


Install the packages from the requirements.txt and run the example script `cli_clock.py`:

```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ pip install -r requirements.txt
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ python cli_clock.py 
02/24/2021 11:20:49
```

The terminal should show the time, you can press `ctrl-c` to exit the script.
If you are unfamiliar with the Python code in `cli_clock.py`, have a look at [this Python refresher](https://hackernoon.com/intermediate-python-refresher-tutorial-project-ideas-and-tips-i28s320p). If you are still concerned, please reach out to the teaching staff!


## Part C. 
### Set up your RGB Display
We have asked you to equip the [Adafruit MiniPiTFT](https://www.adafruit.com/product/4393) on your Pi in the Lab 2 prep already. Here, we will introduce you to the MiniPiTFT and Python scripts on the Pi with more details.

<img src="https://cdn-learn.adafruit.com/assets/assets/000/082/842/large1024/adafruit_products_4393_iso_ORIG_2019_10.jpg" height="200" />

The Raspberry Pi 5 has a variety of interfacing options. When you plug the pi in the red power LED turns on. Any time the SD card is accessed the green LED flashes. It has standard USB ports and HDMI ports. Less familiar it has a set of 20x2 pin headers that allow you to connect a various peripherals.

<img src="https://maker.pro/storage/g9KLAxU/g9KLAxUiJb9e4Zp1xcxrMhbCDyc3QWPdSunYAoew.png" height="400" />

To learn more about any individual pin and what it is for go to [pinout.xyz](https://pinout.xyz/pinout/3v3_power) and click on the pin. Some terms may be unfamiliar but we will go over the relevant ones as they come up.

### Hardware (you have already done this in the prep)

From your kit take out the display and the [Raspberry Pi 5](https://www.google.com/url?sa=i&url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.raspberrypi.com%2Fproducts%2Fraspberry-pi-5%2F&psig=AOvVaw330s4wIQWfHou2Vk3-0jUN&ust=1757611779758000&source=images&cd=vfe&opi=89978449&ved=0CBMQjRxqFwoTCPi1-5_czo8DFQAAAAAdAAAAABAE)

Line up the screen and press it on the headers. The hole in the screen should match up with the hole on the raspberry pi.

<p float="left">
<img src="https://cdn-learn.adafruit.com/assets/assets/000/087/539/medium640/adafruit_products_4393_quarter_ORIG_2019_10.jpg?1579991932" height="200" />
<img src="https://cdn-learn.adafruit.com/assets/assets/000/082/861/original/adafruit_products_image.png" height="200">
</p>

### Testing your Screen

The display uses a communication protocol called [SPI](https://www.circuitbasics.com/basics-of-the-spi-communication-protocol/) to speak with the raspberry pi. We won't go in depth in this course over how SPI works. The port on the bottom of the display connects to the SDA and SCL pins used for the I2C communication protocol which we will cover later. GPIO (General Purpose Input/Output) pins 23 and 24 are connected to the two buttons on the left. GPIO 22 controls the display backlight.

To show you the IP and Mac address of the Pi to allow connecting remotely we created a service that launches a python script that runs on boot. For the following steps stop the service by typing ``` sudo systemctl stop piscreen.service --now```. Othwerise two scripts will try to use the screen at once. You may start it again by typing ``` sudo systemctl start piscreen.service --now```

We can test it by typing 
```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ python screen_test.py
```

You can type the name of a color then press either of the buttons on the MiniPiTFT to see what happens on the display! You can press `ctrl-c` to exit the script. Take a look at the code with
```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ cat screen_test.py
```

#### Displaying Info with Texts
You can look in `screen_boot_script.py` for how to display text on the screen!

#### Displaying an image

You can look in `image.py` for an example of how to display an image on the screen. Can you make it switch to another image when you push one of the buttons?

## Part D. 
### Set up the Display Clock Demo
Work on `screen_clock.py`, try to show the time by filling in the while loop (at the bottom of the script where we noted "TODO" for you). You can use the code in `cli_clock.py` and `stats.py` to figure this out.

### How to Edit Scripts on Pi
Option 1. One of the ways for you to edit scripts on Pi through terminal is using [`nano`](https://linuxize.com/post/how-to-use-nano-text-editor/) command. You can go into the `screen_clock.py` by typing the follow command line:
```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ nano screen_clock.py
```
You can make changes to the script this way, remember to save the changes by pressing `ctrl-o` and press enter again. You can press `ctrl-x` to exit the nano mode. There are more options listed down in the terminal you can use in nano.

Option 2. Another way for you to edit scripts is to use VNC on your laptop to remotely connect your Pi. Try to open the files directly like what you will do with your laptop and edit them. Since the default OS we have for you does not come up a python programmer, you will have to install one yourself otherwise you will have to edit the codes with text editor. [Thonny IDE](https://thonny.org/) is a good option for you to install, try run the following command lines in your Pi's ternimal:

  ```
  pi@raspberrypi:~ $ sudo apt install thonny
  pi@raspberrypi:~ $ sudo apt update && sudo apt upgrade -y
  ```

Now you should be able to edit python scripts with Thonny on your Pi.

Option 3. A nowadays often preferred method is to use Microsoft [VS code to remote connect to the Pi](https://www.raspberrypi.com/news/coding-on-raspberry-pi-remotely-with-visual-studio-code/). This gives you access to a fullly equipped and responsive code editor with terminal and file browser.  

Pro Tip: Using tools like [code-server](https://coder.com/docs/code-server/latest) you can even setup a VS Code coding environment hosted on your raspberry pi and code through a web browser on your tablet or smartphone! 

## Part E. Read Part 2. Sketch and brainstorm further interactions and features you would like for your clock.

One potential source of ideas might be thinking about other clocks and timekeeping devices for inspiration.

Another might be novel units of time. How do you measure a year? [In daylights? In midnights? In cups of coffee?](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wsj15wPpjLY)

We strongly discourage literal digital or analog clock display: Be creative.
[clock_2.html](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/32167591/clock_2.html)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>实时时钟 · Real-Time Clock</title>
<style>
  :root {
    --face: #f7f5ef;
    --rim: #2b2b2b;
    --tick: #1c1c1c;
    --hour: #14161a;
    --minute: #14161a;
    --second: #d62b2b;
    --accent: #d62b2b;
  }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  html, body {
    height: 100%;
    margin: 0;
  }
  body {
    height: 100vh;
    height: 100dvh;
    overflow: hidden;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto;   /* 控制条 / 表盘 / 读数 */
    justify-items: center;
    align-items: center;
    /* 四周留白：随视口大小伸缩 */
    /* 四周留白需大于 CRT 外框宽度，保证边框不压住控件 */
    padding: clamp(22px, 3.0vmin, 46px) clamp(24px, 3.6vmin, 58px);
    column-gap: 0;
    row-gap: clamp(6px, 1.4vmin, 18px);
    background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 35%, #2f3440 0%, #14161b 60%, #0a0b0e 100%);
    color: #e9ecf1;
    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", "Helvetica Neue",
      "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    user-select: none;
  }

  .topbar {
    justify-self: end;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
  }

  /* 表盘舞台：占据读数之外的全部剩余空间 */
  .stage {
    justify-self: stretch;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 0;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    container-type: size;
    --u: 1cqmin;       /* 舞台较小边的 1%，用于等比缩放所有细节 */
  }

  .clock {
    position: relative;
    /* 回退值（不支持容器查询单位时） */
    width: min(78vmin, 560px);
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    /* 表盘直径 = 舞台较小边，即视口减去留白后能放下的最大正方形 */
    width: min(100cqw, 100cqh);
    border-radius: 50%;
    background:
      radial-gradient(circle at 38% 30%, #ffffff 0%, var(--face) 55%, #e3ded2 100%);
    border: clamp(6px, calc(2.1 * var(--u)), 42px) solid var(--rim);
    box-shadow:
      0 0 0 clamp(2px, calc(0.9 * var(--u)), 16px) #6f6f6f,
      0 0 0 clamp(3px, calc(1.35 * var(--u)), 24px) #101114,
      0 calc(2.6 * var(--u)) calc(6 * var(--u)) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65),
      inset 0 0 calc(3.4 * var(--u)) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18),
      inset 0 calc(0.9 * var(--u)) calc(2 * var(--u)) rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  }

  /* 整盘旋转容器：绕表盘圆心旋转，内容贴在顶部 */
  .ring {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    transform-origin: 50% 50%;
  }

  /* 刻度（尺寸按表盘直径等比缩放） */
  .tick {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 2.7%;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    background: var(--tick);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }
  .tick.hour-tick {
    width: 1.55%;
    height: 4.6%;
  }
  .tick.minute-tick {
    width: 0.62%;
    height: 2.5%;
    opacity: 0.72;
  }

  /* 数字：贴在半径约 38% 处，并反向旋转保持正立 */
  .numeral {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 12%;
    font-size: clamp(15px, 4vmin, 28px);          /* 回退值 */
    font-size: clamp(12px, calc(6 * var(--u)), 220px);
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #1a1c20;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  }

  /* 指针 */
  .hand {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    bottom: 50%;
    transform-origin: 50% 100%;
    border-radius: 999px;
    will-change: transform;
  }
  .hand.hour {
    width: 1.9%;
    height: 28%;
    margin-left: -0.95%;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #0e1013 0%, var(--hour) 50%, #3a3f47 100%);
    box-shadow: 0 0 calc(0.7 * var(--u)) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }
  .hand.minute {
    width: 1.35%;
    height: 40%;
    margin-left: -0.675%;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #0e1013 0%, var(--minute) 50%, #3a3f47 100%);
    box-shadow: 0 0 calc(0.7 * var(--u)) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }
  .hand.second {
    width: 0.7%;
    height: 44%;
    margin-left: -0.35%;
    background: var(--second);
    box-shadow: 0 0 calc(0.9 * var(--u)) rgba(214, 43, 43, 0.55);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }
  .hand.second::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    bottom: -14%;
    width: 260%;
    height: 14%;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    background: var(--second);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }

  /* 中心轴帽 */
  .cap {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 50%;
    width: 5.5%;
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%, #6b7078 0%, #23262b 55%, #0d0e11 100%);
    box-shadow: 0 calc(0.25 * var(--u)) calc(0.7 * var(--u)) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  }
  .cap::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 50%;
    width: 34%;
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: var(--second);
  }

  /* 玻璃反光 */
  .glass {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    border-radius: 50%;
    pointer-events: none;
    background: linear-gradient(150deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.55) 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.06) 34%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 55%);
    mix-blend-mode: screen;
  }

  .readout {
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: clamp(2px, 0.6vmin, 8px);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .digital {
    font-size: clamp(18px, 3.4vmin, 46px);
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 2px;
  }
  .digital .ms {
    font-size: 0.55em;
    opacity: 0.65;
    margin-left: 0.3em;
    letter-spacing: 1px;
  }
  .date {
    font-size: clamp(11px, 1.7vmin, 22px);
    opacity: 0.62;
    letter-spacing: 1px;
  }
  /* 停表时正常时间变暗，日期转红标出"已暂停" */
  body.paused .digital { opacity: 0.5; }
  body.paused .date { opacity: 0.85; color: #ffb4b4; }
  body.paused .hand { transition: none; }

  /* 正常时间与"失步时间"同排：失步计时贴在正常时间右侧 */
  .time-row {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: clamp(7px, 1.5vmin, 20px);
  }

  /* 失步时间：累计"脱离正常时间流"的时长，用调色板中的橙红色标出 */
  .stop-timer {
    display: none;
    align-items: center;
    gap: clamp(5px, 0.95vmin, 12px);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    letter-spacing: 1px;
    color: var(--accent);
  }
  body.paused .stop-timer { display: inline-flex; }
  .stop-timer .oob-icon {
    flex: none;
    width: clamp(15px, 2.5vmin, 32px);
    height: clamp(15px, 2.5vmin, 32px);
    color: var(--accent);
    /* 与 CRT 读数一致的轻微色散：红/青双向偏移（外发光 + 偏移残影） */
    filter:
      drop-shadow(0 0 clamp(4px, 0.9vmin, 12px) rgba(214, 43, 43, 0.7))
      drop-shadow(-1.5px 0 rgba(255, 84, 84, 0.45))
      drop-shadow(1.5px 0 rgba(84, 200, 255, 0.45));
  }
  .stop-timer .val {
    color: #ffb4b4;                                    /* 回退 */
    color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent), #ffb4b4 58%);
    font-size: clamp(13px, 2.1vmin, 26px);
    font-weight: 700;
    /* 与 CRT 读数一致的轻微色散：红/青双向偏移 */
    text-shadow:
      0 0 clamp(6px, 1.2vmin, 18px) rgba(214, 43, 43, 0.6),
      -1.5px 0 rgba(255, 84, 84, 0.45),
      1.5px 0 rgba(84, 200, 255, 0.45);
  }
  /* 毫秒段：沿用主时钟 .ms 的弱化处理，保持整行不跳动 */
  .stop-timer .val .ms {
    font-size: 0.6em;
    font-weight: 600;
    opacity: 0.7;
    margin-left: 0.15em;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  }

  /* 控制按钮：精致的单色线性图标 */
  .ctl-group {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: clamp(8px, 1.2vmin, 14px);
  }
  .ctl {
    --ctl-size: clamp(44px, 5.2vmin, 54px);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    width: var(--ctl-size);
    height: var(--ctl-size);
    padding: 0;
    color: #eef1f6;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
    border-radius: 50%;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
    backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14),
      0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.18s ease, border-color 0.18s ease,
      box-shadow 0.18s ease, transform 0.18s ease;
  }
  .ctl .ico {
    width: 1.5em;
    height: 1.5em;
    overflow: visible;
    fill: none;
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 1.7;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
  }
  .ctl:hover:not(:disabled) {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.13);
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
  }
  .ctl:active:not(:disabled) { transform: translateY(0); }
  .ctl[aria-pressed="true"] {
    color: #fff;
    background: rgba(214, 43, 43, 0.9);
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35),
      0 0 16px rgba(214, 43, 43, 0.5);
  }
  .ctl[aria-pressed="true"]:hover { background: rgba(230, 60, 60, 1); }
  .ctl:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid #7aa7ff;
    outline-offset: 3px;
  }
  .ctl:disabled {
    opacity: 0.3;
    cursor: not-allowed;
    box-shadow: none;
    transform: none;
  }
  .ctl .mute { display: none; }
  .ctl[aria-pressed="false"] .mute { display: inline; }
  .ctl[aria-pressed="true"] .waves { display: inline; }

  /* ============================================================
     CRT 显像管效果
     整层 CRT 只在"停表"时出现：走时中完全不施加任何着色，画面保持干净；
     停表后玻璃外框、荫罩、扫描线、暗角与静电一起淡入。
     两个全屏 WebGL 画布叠在时钟之上（不拦截指针）：
       #crtGlow  screen   — 荧光晕、滚屏亮带、静电亮点（加光）
       #crtMask  multiply — 荫罩 RGB 子像素、扫描线、暗角、噪点与抖动（减光）
     无 WebGL 时由 body.no-gl.paused 的 CSS 扫描线兜底。
     ============================================================ */
  #crtGlow,
  #crtMask {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: block;
    pointer-events: none;
    opacity: 0;                       /* 走时中：整层关闭，不施加任何着色 */
    transition: opacity 0.34s ease;
  }
  #crtGlow { z-index: 40; mix-blend-mode: screen; }
  #crtMask { z-index: 41; mix-blend-mode: multiply; }
  body.paused #crtGlow,
  body.paused #crtMask { opacity: 1; }

  /* 屏幕玻璃：圆角、显像管外框、内嵌暗角与高光 */
  .crt-screen {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 50;
    pointer-events: none;
    opacity: 0;                       /* 与着色层同步：停表时才亮起显像管外框 */
    transition: opacity 0.34s ease;
    border-radius: clamp(20px, 4.4vmin, 46px);
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 0 0 clamp(8px, 1.8vmin, 20px) rgba(3, 4, 6, 0.94),
      inset 0 0 0 clamp(9px, 2.0vmin, 22px) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85),
      inset 0 0 clamp(52px, 11vmin, 150px) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5),
      inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  }
  .crt-screen::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    border-radius: inherit;
    background:
      linear-gradient(133deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02) 20%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 42%),
      linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 12%);
    mix-blend-mode: screen;
  }
  body.paused .crt-screen { opacity: 1; }

  /* 读数：多圈荧光晕 + 轻微色散，让数字成为高亮发光体 */
  .digital {
    color: #f4f8ff;
    text-shadow:
      0 0 clamp(5px, 0.9vmin, 12px) rgba(235, 244, 255, 0.75),
      0 0 clamp(13px, 2.4vmin, 36px) rgba(160, 214, 255, 0.8),
      0 0 clamp(28px, 5.6vmin, 84px) rgba(112, 180, 255, 0.5),
      0 0 clamp(56px, 11vmin, 160px) rgba(80, 150, 255, 0.28),
      -1.5px 0 rgba(255, 84, 84, 0.45),
      1.5px 0 rgba(84, 200, 255, 0.45);
  }
  .date {
    text-shadow: 0 0 clamp(8px, 1.4vmin, 20px) rgba(150, 210, 255, 0.35);
  }

  /* WebGL 不可用时的纯 CSS 扫描线 + 暗角（同样只在停表时出现） */
  body.no-gl.paused::after {
    content: "";
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 41;
    pointer-events: none;
    background:
      repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 0, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 1px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 1px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 3px),
      radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 45%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 42%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55) 100%);
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="topbar">
    <div class="ctl-group" data-od-id="clock-controls">
      <button class="ctl" id="stopBtn" type="button"
              title="停止计时：指针保持不动（快捷键 S）" aria-label="停止计时">
        <svg class="ico" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <line x1="9" y1="6.4" x2="9" y2="17.6" />
          <line x1="15" y1="6.4" x2="15" y2="17.6" />
        </svg>
      </button>
      <button class="ctl" id="resumeBtn" type="button" disabled
              title="继续计时：从停下的时刻接着走（快捷键 R）" aria-label="继续计时">
        <svg class="ico" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M8.6 6.3 17.6 12 8.6 17.7Z" />
        </svg>
      </button>
      <button class="ctl" id="soundToggle" type="button" aria-pressed="false"
              title="开启滴答声（快捷键 M）" aria-label="开启滴答声">
        <svg class="ico" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M4.6 9.3h2.6L11 6.5v11l-3.8-2.8H4.6z" />
          <g class="waves">
            <path d="M14.4 9.3a4 4 0 0 1 0 5.4" />
            <path d="M17.1 7.1a7.3 7.3 0 0 1 0 9.8" />
          </g>
          <g class="mute">
            <line x1="5.2" y1="19" x2="19" y2="5" />
          </g>
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="stage" id="stage">
    <div class="clock" id="clock" role="img" aria-label="实时模拟时钟">
      <div class="glass"></div>
      <div class="cap"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="readout">
    <div class="time-row">
      <div class="digital" id="digital">--:--:--<span class="ms">.000</span></div>
      <div class="stop-timer" id="stopTimer" data-od-id="out-of-sync-timer"
           role="timer" aria-label="失步时间" title="失步时间：脱离正常时间流的时长">
        <svg class="oob-icon" viewBox="0 -960 960 960" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
          <path fill="currentColor" d="M120-160v-80h110l-16-14q-52-46-73-105t-21-119q0-111 66.5-197.5T360-790v84q-72 26-116 88.5T200-478q0 45 17 87.5t53 78.5l10 10v-98h80v240H120Zm331.5-131.5Q440-303 440-320t11.5-28.5Q463-360 480-360t28.5 11.5Q520-337 520-320t-11.5 28.5Q497-280 480-280t-28.5-11.5ZM440-440v-240h80v240h-80Zm160 270v-84q72-26 116-88.5T760-482q0-45-17-87.5T690-648l-10-10v98h-80v-240h240v80H730l16 14q49 49 71.5 106.5T840-482q0 111-66.5 197.5T600-170Z"/>
        </svg>
        <span class="val" id="stopTimerVal"><span id="stopTimerBase">00:00</span><span class="ms" id="stopTimerMs">.000</span></span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="date" id="date">—</div>
  </div>

  <!-- CRT 叠加层：先加光，再减光，最后是玻璃外框 -->
  <canvas id="crtGlow" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
  <canvas id="crtMask" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
  <div class="crt-screen" data-od-id="crt-screen" aria-hidden="true"></div>

<script>
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var clockEl = document.getElementById("clock");
  var digitalEl = document.getElementById("digital");
  var dateEl = document.getElementById("date");
  var stopTimerBaseEl = document.getElementById("stopTimerBase");
  var stopTimerMsEl = document.getElementById("stopTimerMs");

  /* ---------- 构建表盘：60 个刻度 + 12 个数字 ---------- */
  function buildFace() {
    var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();

    // 60 个刻度
    for (var i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
      var isHour = i % 5 === 0;
      var ring = document.createElement("div");
      ring.className = "ring";
      ring.style.transform = "rotate(" + i * 6 + "deg)";  // 0deg = 12 点方向

      var tick = document.createElement("div");
      tick.className = "tick " + (isHour ? "hour-tick" : "minute-tick");
      ring.appendChild(tick);
      frag.appendChild(ring);
    }

    // 12 个数字
    for (var n = 1; n <= 12; n++) {
      var angle = n * 30;
      var numRing = document.createElement("div");
      numRing.className = "ring";
      numRing.style.transform = "rotate(" + angle + "deg)";

      var label = document.createElement("div");
      label.className = "numeral";
      label.textContent = String(n);
      // 居中于 12% 处，再反向旋转抵消容器旋转，使数字始终正立
      label.style.transform =
        "translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(" + (-angle) + "deg)";
      numRing.appendChild(label);
      frag.appendChild(numRing);
    }

    var cap = clockEl.querySelector(".cap");
    clockEl.insertBefore(frag, cap);
  }

  /* ---------- 指针元素 ---------- */
  function createHand(cls) {
    var el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = "hand " + cls;
    clockEl.insertBefore(el, clockEl.querySelector(".cap"));
    return el;
  }

  buildFace();
  var hourHand = createHand("hour");
  var minuteHand = createHand("minute");
  var secondHand = createHand("second");

  var WEEKDAYS = ["星期日", "星期一", "星期二", "星期三", "星期四", "星期五", "星期六"];
  var pad = function (v, w) {
    return String(v).padStart(w || 2, "0");
  };

  /* ---------- 计时时钟：停止/继续后不再与真实时间对齐 ----------
     显示时间 = 计时原点 - offsetMs。停表时时间源本身冻结在停下的那一刻；
     继续时把停顿的时长累加进 offsetMs，于是从停下的那一刻接着走，
     与真实时间保持一个固定偏差（不"跳回"真实时间）。 */
  var virtualClock = (function () {
    var offsetMs = 0;
    var running = true;
    var stoppedAt = 0;
    function base() { return running ? Date.now() : stoppedAt; }
    return {
      nowMs: function () { return base() - offsetMs; },
      date: function () { return new Date(base() - offsetMs); },
      isRunning: function () { return running; },
      offsetMs: function () { return offsetMs; },
      pausedMs: function () { return running ? 0 : (Date.now() - stoppedAt); },
      stop: function () {
        if (!running) return;
        stoppedAt = Date.now();
        running = false;
      },
      resume: function () {
        if (running) return;
        offsetMs += Date.now() - stoppedAt;
        running = true;
      }
    };
  })();

  /* ---------- 停表"挣扎"时间线：秒针向前挣、被弹回，一次比一次无力 ----------
     动画与音效共用这一份时序，保证声画同步。 */
  var STOP_PULLS = [
    { at: 0.06, dur: 0.50, reach: 0.95, jitter: 1.00 },
    { at: 0.72, dur: 0.52, reach: 0.70, jitter: 0.80 },
    { at: 1.40, dur: 0.54, reach: 0.48, jitter: 0.60 },
    { at: 2.12, dur: 0.58, reach: 0.30, jitter: 0.40 },
    { at: 2.88, dur: 0.62, reach: 0.16, jitter: 0.25 },
    { at: 3.62, dur: 0.46, reach: 0.08, jitter: 0.15 }
  ];
  var PUSH_RATIO = 0.34;      // 一次挣扎中"往前挣"所占的时间比例
  var MIN_PUSH_DEG = 3.2;     // 即便正好停在刻度上，也要挣出看得见的角度

  /* 一次挣扎的角度：先缓出地往前挣（带吃力抖动），再阻尼回弹甚至越过原点 */
  function pullAngle(tl, pull, gap) {
    var amt = Math.max(gap * pull.reach, MIN_PUSH_DEG * pull.reach);
    if (tl < PUSH_RATIO) {
      var u = tl / PUSH_RATIO;
      var ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - u, 3);
      return amt * ease + pull.jitter * 0.7 * Math.sin(u * 42) * u * (1 - u);
    }
    var v = (tl - PUSH_RATIO) / (1 - PUSH_RATIO);
    return amt * Math.exp(-3.4 * v) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * 1.5 * v);
  }

  function prefersReducedMotion() {
    return !!(window.matchMedia &&
      window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches);
  }

  /* ---------- 滴答声：播放内嵌的真实钟表录音（base64，保持单文件） ---------- */
  var clockAudio = (function () {
    var AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    var ctx = null;
    var master = null;
    var sample = null;      // 解码后的录音
    var slices = null;      // 从录音中定位到的两击：{start, dur, gain}
    var useSample = false;  // 录音已就绪
    var sampleFailed = false;  // 录音不可用（解码失败/定位失败）时才退回合成音
    var b64 = null;
    var enabled = false;
    var held = false;       // 停表期间挂起排程
    var timer = null;
    var nextSecond = 0;     // 下一个待发声的整秒（Unix 秒）
    var ticks = 0;
    var LOOKAHEAD = 0.15;   // 提前排程的时间窗（秒）
    var INTERVAL = 25;      // 排程器轮询间隔（毫秒）
    var SLICE = 0.35;       // 每击播放的时长（秒）
    var TARGET_PEAK = 0.6;  // 归一化峰值（原始录音峰值仅约 -18dBFS）

    /* 内嵌录音：<script id="tickSample"> 中的 base64，读取时去掉换行 */
    function sampleBase64() {
      if (b64 !== null) return b64;
      var el = document.getElementById("tickSample");
      b64 = el ? el.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, "") : "";
      return b64;
    }

    function init() {
      if (ctx || !AudioCtx) return !!ctx;
      ctx = new AudioCtx();
      master = ctx.createGain();
      master.gain.value = 0.85;
      master.connect(ctx.destination);
      loadSample();
      return true;
    }

    /* 解码内嵌录音；成功后从波形里定位每一声的起振点 */
    function loadSample() {
      var data = sampleBase64();
      if (!data) { sampleFailed = true; return; }
      var bytes, i;
      try { bytes = atob(data); } catch (e) { sampleFailed = true; return; }
      var buf = new Uint8Array(bytes.length);
      for (i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) buf[i] = bytes.charCodeAt(i);

      var done = false;
      var ok = function (audioBuffer) {
        if (done) return;
        done = true;
        try { prepareSample(audioBuffer); } catch (e) { sampleFailed = true; }
      };
      var fail = function () { sampleFailed = true; };
      try {
        // 同时兼容 Promise 与回调两种接口
        var p = ctx.decodeAudioData(buf.buffer, ok, fail);
        if (p && p.then) p.then(ok).catch(fail);
      } catch (e) { sampleFailed = true; }
    }

    /* 找起点：静音之后第一次越过阈值，即为一击的开始。
       这样定位与解码器是否补齐编码器延迟无关。 */
    function prepareSample(buffer) {
      if (sample) return;
      var data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      var sr = buffer.sampleRate;
      var peak = 0, i, a;
      for (i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
        a = data[i] < 0 ? -data[i] : data[i];
        if (a > peak) peak = a;
      }
      if (peak <= 0.0005) { sampleFailed = true; return; }

      var thr = peak * 0.06;
      var gap = Math.floor(sr * 0.25);      // 两击之间的最小间隔
      var found = [];
      var last = -gap;
      for (i = 0; i < data.length && found.length < 2; i++) {
        a = data[i] < 0 ? -data[i] : data[i];
        if (a > thr && i - last > gap) {
          found.push({ start: Math.max(0, i - Math.floor(sr * 0.002)) });
          last = i;
        }
      }
      if (found.length < 2) { sampleFailed = true; return; }   // 定位失败则退回合成音

      for (i = 0; i < found.length; i++) {
        var s0 = found[i].start;
        var dur = Math.min(SLICE, buffer.duration - s0 / sr);
        found[i].dur = dur;
        var end = Math.min(data.length, s0 + Math.floor(dur * sr));
        var sp = 0, w;
        for (var k = s0; k < end; k++) {
          w = data[k] < 0 ? -data[k] : data[k];
          if (w > sp) sp = w;
        }
        found[i].gain = sp > 0.001 ? TARGET_PEAK / sp : 1;
      }
      sample = buffer;
      slices = found;
      useSample = true;
    }

    /* 播放录音中的一击：从定位到的起振点起取 SLICE 秒 */
    function playSlice(t, index) {
      var sl = slices[index % slices.length];
      var src = ctx.createBufferSource();
      src.buffer = sample;
      var g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(sl.gain, t);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(sl.gain, t + Math.max(0, sl.dur - 0.02));  // 末尾 20ms 淡出
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + sl.dur);
      src.connect(g);
      g.connect(master);
      src.start(t, sl.start / sample.sampleRate, sl.dur);
    }

    /* 兜底：录音不可用时现场合成一声"咔哒" */
    var noise = null;
    function playSynth(t, tock) {
      if (!noise) {
        var len = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * 0.04));
        noise = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
        var d = noise.getChannelData(0);
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
          d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / len, 2.5);
        }
      }
      var click = ctx.createBufferSource();
      click.buffer = noise;
      var band = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      band.type = "bandpass";
      band.frequency.value = tock ? 2100 : 2900;
      band.Q.value = 0.9;
      var cg = ctx.createGain();
      cg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      cg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.9, t + 0.0015);
      cg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.03);
      click.connect(band);
      band.connect(cg);
      cg.connect(master);
      click.start(t);
      click.stop(t + 0.05);

      var body = ctx.createOscillator();
      body.type = "sine";
      body.frequency.setValueAtTime(tock ? 168 : 205, t);
      var bg = ctx.createGain();
      bg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      bg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.25, t + 0.002);
      bg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.045);
      body.connect(bg);
      bg.connect(master);
      body.start(t);
      body.stop(t + 0.07);
    }

    function strike(t, tock) {
      if (useSample) {
        playSlice(t, tock ? 1 : 0);
      } else if (sampleFailed) {
        playSynth(t, tock);          // 录音不可用：退回内置合成音
      } else {
        return;                      // 录音仍在解码：这一声跳过，避免音色突变
      }
      ticks++;
    }

    /* 把"第 sec 个整秒"换算成音频时钟上的时刻。
       每次都从计时时钟重新换算，而不是累加 1 秒——这样长时间运行
       也不会因声卡时钟与系统时钟的速率差而累积漂移。 */
    function audioTimeOf(sec) {
      return ctx.currentTime + (sec * 1000 - virtualClock.nowMs()) / 1000;
    }

    function pump() {
      if (held || !ctx || ctx.state !== "running") return;   // 停表/挂起期间不排程
      var nowAudio = ctx.currentTime;
      var nowMs = virtualClock.nowMs();

      // 页面被挂起/节流后，锚点会落后，重新对齐到下一个整秒
      if (nextSecond * 1000 < nowMs - 150) {
        nextSecond = Math.ceil((nowMs + 5) / 1000);
      }

      var horizon = nowAudio + (document.hidden ? 2 : LOOKAHEAD);
      var guard = 0;
      while (guard++ < 10) {
        var target = audioTimeOf(nextSecond);
        if (target >= horizon) break;
        if (target > nowAudio - 0.02) strike(target, nextSecond % 2 === 0);
        nextSecond += 1;
      }
    }

    /* 停表 / 继续：停表时不排程，继续时从新的计时原点重新对齐 */
    function hold(on) {
      held = !!on;
      if (held) {
        if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
      } else if (enabled) {
        nextSecond = 0;                  // 触发重新对齐
        if (!timer) timer = setInterval(pump, INTERVAL);
        pump();
      }
    }

    function enable() {
      if (!init()) return false;
      if (ctx.state === "suspended") {
        var p = ctx.resume();
        if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {});
      }
      nextSecond = 0;                    // 触发首次对齐
      if (!held) {
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
        timer = setInterval(pump, INTERVAL);
        pump();
      }
      enabled = true;
      return true;
    }

    function disable() {
      enabled = false;
      if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
      if (ctx && ctx.state === "running") {
        var p = ctx.suspend();
        if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {});
      }
    }

    /* ---------- 停表挣扎的音效：与动画共用 STOP_PULLS 的时序 ---------- */

    var hiss = null;      // 长噪声，用于吱嘎声
    var drama = [];       // 正在进行的挣扎音源，便于"继续"时立刻叫停

    function track(node, endAt) {
      node.__end = endAt;
      drama.push(node);
      return node;
    }

    /* 清掉已经播完的节点：持续挣扎会一直排新声音，不清会无限增长 */
    function pruneDrama() {
      if (!ctx) return;
      var now = ctx.currentTime;
      for (var i = drama.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (drama[i].__end < now) drama.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }

    function hissBuffer() {
      if (!hiss) {
        var len = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * 0.5));
        hiss = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
        var d = hiss.getChannelData(0);
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
      }
      return hiss;
    }

    /* 半声滴答：把录音那一击拽慢，音高与音量一起塌下去——想走却没走成 */
    function stuntedTick(t, k) {
      if (useSample) {
        var sl = slices[0];
        var src = ctx.createBufferSource();
        src.buffer = sample;
        src.playbackRate.setValueAtTime(0.95, t);
        src.playbackRate.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.45, t + 0.20);
        var g = ctx.createGain();
        g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
        g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.75 * k, t + 0.004);
        g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.22);
        src.connect(g);
        g.connect(master);
        src.start(t, sl.start / sample.sampleRate, sl.dur);
        src.stop(t + 0.24);
        track(src, t + 0.24);
        return;
      }
      var sq = ctx.createOscillator();
      sq.type = "square";
      sq.frequency.setValueAtTime(1900, t);
      sq.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(700, t + 0.18);
      var sg = ctx.createGain();
      sg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      sg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.14 * k, t + 0.004);
      sg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.20);
      sq.connect(sg);
      sg.connect(master);
      sq.start(t);
      sq.stop(t + 0.22);
      track(sq, t + 0.22);
    }

    /* 越挣越紧的吱嘎：带通噪声的共振点一路上扬 */
    function strain(t, dur, k) {
      var src = ctx.createBufferSource();
      src.buffer = hissBuffer();
      src.loop = true;
      var bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      bp.type = "bandpass";
      bp.Q.value = 7;
      bp.frequency.setValueAtTime(300, t);
      bp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1600, t + dur);
      var g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.09 * k, t + dur * 0.92);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
      src.connect(bp);
      bp.connect(g);
      g.connect(master);
      src.start(t);
      src.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
      track(src, t + dur + 0.02);
    }

    /* 被弹回来的"boing"：三角波音高急速下坠 */
    function boing(t, k) {
      var osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(185, t);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(56, t + 0.17);
      var g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.26 * k, t + 0.006);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.21);
      osc.connect(g);
      g.connect(master);
      osc.start(t);
      osc.stop(t + 0.23);
      track(osc, t + 0.23);
    }

    /* 机构卡死的闷响 */
    function clunk(t) {
      var osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      osc.type = "sine";
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(150, t);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(48, t + 0.13);
      var g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.004);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.19);
      osc.connect(g);
      g.connect(master);
      osc.start(t);
      osc.stop(t + 0.21);
      track(osc, t + 0.21);
    }

    /* 一次挣扎之声：越挣越紧 → 没走成的半声滴答 → 弹簧回弹
       delay 是距离该次挣扎还有多久（秒），由动画的时间线给出 */
    function struggle(delay, pushDur, k) {
      if (!ctx || !enabled || ctx.state !== "running") return;
      pruneDrama();
      var t = ctx.currentTime + Math.max(0.005, delay);
      var push = Math.max(0.06, pushDur * 0.95);
      strain(t, push, k);
      stuntedTick(t + push, Math.max(0.26, k));
      boing(t + push, Math.max(0.20, k));
    }

    /* 停表瞬间：机构卡死 */
    function struggleStart() {
      if (!ctx || !enabled || ctx.state !== "running") return;
      cancelDrama();
      clunk(ctx.currentTime + 0.03);
    }

    function cancelDrama() {
      for (var i = 0; i < drama.length; i++) {
        try { drama[i].stop(0); } catch (e) {}
        try { drama[i].disconnect(); } catch (e) {}
      }
      drama.length = 0;
    }

    return {
      supported: !!AudioCtx,
      isEnabled: function () { return enabled; },
      tickCount: function () { return ticks; },
      enable: enable,
      disable: disable,
      hold: hold,
      struggle: struggle,
      struggleStart: struggleStart,
      cancelDrama: cancelDrama,
      isHeld: function () { return held; },
      toggle: function () {
        if (enabled) disable(); else enable();
        return enabled;
      },
      /* 调试/自动化检查用 */
      debug: function () {
        return {
          source: useSample ? "recording" : "synth",
          sampleDuration: sample ? +sample.duration.toFixed(3) : null,
          slices: slices ? slices.map(function (s) {
            return { start: +(s.start / sample.sampleRate).toFixed(4), dur: +s.dur.toFixed(3), gain: +s.gain.toFixed(2) };
          }) : null,
          liveDramaNodes: drama.length      // 正在发声的挣扎音源（应保持很小）
        };
      }
    };
  })();

  /* ---------- 控制按钮：停止 / 继续 / 声音 ---------- */
  var toggleBtn = document.getElementById("soundToggle");
  var stopBtn = document.getElementById("stopBtn");
  var resumeBtn = document.getElementById("resumeBtn");

  function syncSoundUI() {
    var on = clockAudio.isEnabled();
    var label = !on
      ? "开启滴答声"
      : (virtualClock.isRunning() ? "滴答声已开启" : "滴答声已暂停");
    toggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", on ? "true" : "false");
    toggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
    toggleBtn.setAttribute("title", label + "（快捷键 M）");
  }

  function syncButtons() {
    var running = virtualClock.isRunning();
    document.body.classList.toggle("paused", !running);
    stopBtn.disabled = !running;
    resumeBtn.disabled = running;
    // 停表时动画循环已停，按钮状态变化后需要补一帧
    if (!running) render();
    syncSoundUI();
  }

  /* ---------- 失步时间：从停下的那一刻起累计脱离正常时间流的时长 ---------- */
  function formatStopwatch(ms) {
    var total = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    var s = total % 60;
    var m = Math.floor(total / 60) % 60;
    var h = Math.floor(total / 3600);
    return (h > 0 ? h + ":" + pad(m) + ":" : pad(m) + ":") + pad(s);
  }

  var stopTimerId = 0;
  var oobIconEl = document.querySelector(".stop-timer .oob-icon");
  var lastBlinkSecond = -1;

  /* 图标与读数同拍：每当读数进位一秒，图标闪一下（同 CRT 荧光衰减的节奏）。
     毫秒读数逐帧刷新，但闪烁仍锚定"秒"这一可读节拍，避免 1kHz 频闪。 */
  function blinkOobIcon() {
    if (!oobIconEl || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
    if (typeof oobIconEl.animate !== "function") return;
    oobIconEl.animate(
      [
        { opacity: 1, offset: 0 },
        { opacity: 0.28, offset: 0.38 },
        { opacity: 1, offset: 1 }
      ],
      { duration: 300, easing: "ease-out" }
    );
  }

  function updateStopTimer() {
    if (!stopTimerBaseEl) return;
    var elapsed = virtualClock.pausedMs();
    stopTimerBaseEl.textContent = formatStopwatch(elapsed);
    if (stopTimerMsEl) stopTimerMsEl.textContent = "." + pad(Math.floor(elapsed % 1000), 3);
    var sec = Math.floor(elapsed / 1000);
    if (sec !== lastBlinkSecond) {
      lastBlinkSecond = sec;
      blinkOobIcon();
    }
  }
  /* 用 rAF 逐帧刷新，毫秒位才会连续滚动（200ms 定时器只能显示到 0.2s 量级） */
  function stopTimerFrame() {
    updateStopTimer();
    stopTimerId = requestAnimationFrame(stopTimerFrame);
  }
  function startStopTimer() {
    lastBlinkSecond = -1;
    updateStopTimer();
    if (!stopTimerId) stopTimerId = requestAnimationFrame(stopTimerFrame);
  }
  function stopStopTimer() {
    if (stopTimerId) { cancelAnimationFrame(stopTimerId); stopTimerId = 0; }
    lastBlinkSecond = -1;
  }

  function stopClock() {
    if (!virtualClock.isRunning()) return;
    virtualClock.stop();
    clockAudio.hold(true);   // 停表就不该再滴答
    render();                // 补最后一帧（含"已暂停"标记）
    stopRenderLoop();        // 停表后不再逐帧重绘
    syncButtons();
    startStopTimer();            // 开始累计停表时长
    clockAudio.struggleStart();  // 机构卡死的闷响
    startStopAnimation();        // 秒针开始挣扎：开场递减，之后永远持续（音效同源排程）
  }

  function resumeClock() {
    if (virtualClock.isRunning()) return;
    cancelStopAnimation();
    clockAudio.cancelDrama();
    stopStopTimer();             // 恢复计时即清空失步时间
    virtualClock.resume();
    clockAudio.hold(false);
    startRenderLoop();
    syncButtons();
  }

  /* ---------- 停表动画：秒针徒劳地往前挣、被弹回，永不停歇 ----------
     开场是六次递减的爆发；之后转入持续挣扎：每一"秒"再试一次，
     幅度缓慢起伏、偶尔用力一点，但永不归零。音效与动画共用这条时间线。 */
  var STRUGGLE_STEP = 1.0;       // 持续挣扎的节拍：每秒再试一次
  var SOUND_LOOKAHEAD = 0.30;    // 音效提前排程窗口（秒）
  var struggle = null;

  /* 持续阶段的一次挣扎：幅度在 0.2~0.5 间缓慢起伏，永不归零 */
  function makePull(at, index) {
    var reach = 0.30 + 0.10 * Math.sin(index * 0.85) + 0.04 * Math.sin(index * 2.7);
    if (index % 9 === 0) reach += 0.16;          // 偶尔挣扎得用力一点
    return {
      at: at,
      dur: 0.62,
      reach: Math.max(0.18, reach),
      jitter: 0.22,
      sound: false
    };
  }

  function startStopAnimation() {
    cancelStopAnimation();
    var d = virtualClock.date();
    var secs = d.getSeconds() + d.getMilliseconds() / 1000;
    var frac = secs - Math.floor(secs);
    var baseAngle = secs * 6;                 // 停下的角度
    var gap = (1 - frac) * 6;                 // 到下一格刻度的角距

    if (prefersReducedMotion()) {             // 尊重"减少动态效果"
      secondHand.style.transform = "rotate(" + baseAngle + "deg)";
      return;
    }

    var last = STOP_PULLS[STOP_PULLS.length - 1];
    var openingEnd = last.at + last.dur;
    var firstTick = 1 - frac;                 // 停表后的第一个"整秒"

    var st = {
      t0: performance.now(),
      baseAngle: baseAngle,
      gap: gap,
      grid: firstTick,                        // 持续挣扎的节拍原点
      pulls: STOP_PULLS.slice(),              // 开场六次爆发
      nextAt: firstTick,
      index: 0,
      raf: 0
    };
    // 开场结束后才交给持续挣扎
    while (st.nextAt < openingEnd + 0.2) st.nextAt += STRUGGLE_STEP;
    struggle = st;

    function step(now) {
      var el = (now - st.t0) / 1000;

      // 页面被挂起后重新对齐节拍，避免一次补出成百上千次挣扎
      if (st.nextAt < el - 1) {
        st.nextAt = st.grid + Math.ceil((el - st.grid) / STRUGGLE_STEP) * STRUGGLE_STEP;
      }
      // 生成接下来的挣扎（始终保留约 2 秒余量，于是永不停止）
      while (st.nextAt < el + 2) {
        st.pulls.push(makePull(st.nextAt, st.index));
        st.index++;
        st.nextAt += STRUGGLE_STEP;
      }
      // 丢掉早已结束的
      while (st.pulls.length > 2 && st.pulls[0].at + st.pulls[0].dur < el - 0.5) st.pulls.shift();

      // 为即将开始的挣扎排声音：与动画同一条时间线，声画同源
      for (var i = 0; i < st.pulls.length; i++) {
        var p = st.pulls[i];
        if (!p.sound && (p.at - el) <= SOUND_LOOKAHEAD) {
          p.sound = true;
          if (p.at - el > -0.05) clockAudio.struggle(p.at - el, p.dur * PUSH_RATIO, p.reach);
        }
      }

      // 画指针
      var off = 0;
      for (i = 0; i < st.pulls.length; i++) {
        var q = st.pulls[i];
        if (el >= q.at && el <= q.at + q.dur) {
          off = pullAngle((el - q.at) / q.dur, q, st.gap);
          break;
        }
      }
      secondHand.style.transform = "rotate(" + (st.baseAngle + off) + "deg)";
      st.raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    st.raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  function cancelStopAnimation() {
    if (struggle && struggle.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(struggle.raf);
    struggle = null;
  }


  if (!clockAudio.supported) {
    toggleBtn.disabled = true;
    toggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-label", "此浏览器不支持音频");
    toggleBtn.setAttribute("title", "此浏览器不支持音频");
  } else {
    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
      clockAudio.toggle();
      syncSoundUI();
    });
  }
  syncSoundUI();

  stopBtn.addEventListener("click", stopClock);
  resumeBtn.addEventListener("click", resumeClock);

  document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
    var k = e.key;
    if (k === "m" || k === "M") {
      if (clockAudio.supported) { clockAudio.toggle(); syncSoundUI(); }
    } else if (k === "s" || k === "S") {
      stopClock();
    } else if (k === "r" || k === "R") {
      resumeClock();
    }
  });

  // 调试/自动化钩子（不影响页面行为）
  window.__clockAudio = clockAudio;
  window.__virtualClock = virtualClock;

  /* ---------- 每帧按计时时钟更新时间与角度 ---------- */
  var rafId = 0;

  function render() {
    var now = virtualClock.date();

    var ms = now.getMilliseconds();
    var s = now.getSeconds();
    var m = now.getMinutes();
    var h = now.getHours();

    // 秒针连续扫动（含毫秒），分/时针也按比例平滑推进
    var seconds = s + ms / 1000;
    var minutes = m + seconds / 60;
    var hours = (h % 12) + minutes / 60;

    hourHand.style.transform   = "rotate(" + (hours * 30) + "deg)";
    minuteHand.style.transform = "rotate(" + (minutes * 6) + "deg)";
    secondHand.style.transform = "rotate(" + (seconds * 6) + "deg)";

    digitalEl.innerHTML =
      pad(h) + ":" + pad(m) + ":" + pad(s) +
      '<span class="ms">.' + pad(ms, 3) + "</span>";

    dateEl.textContent =
      now.getFullYear() + " 年 " + (now.getMonth() + 1) + " 月 " + now.getDate() + " 日 · " +
      WEEKDAYS[now.getDay()] +
      (virtualClock.isRunning() ? "" : " · 已暂停");
  }

  function frame() {
    render();
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function startRenderLoop() {
    if (rafId) return;
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function stopRenderLoop() {
    if (rafId) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = 0; }
  }

  document.body.classList.toggle("paused", !virtualClock.isRunning());
  syncButtons();
  startRenderLoop();
})();
</script>

  <!-- ============================================================
       CRT 显像管效果（GPU）
       两遍片元着色器分别做"加光"与"减光"，叠加在时钟之上。
       仅在停表（body.paused）时绘制并淡入；走时中不施加任何着色。
       单文件、零外部请求；优先 WebGL2，退化到 WebGL1；都不可用时
       由 body.no-gl.paused 的 CSS 扫描线兜底，时钟本身始终可用。
       ============================================================ -->
  <script>
  (function () {
    "use strict";

    var maskCanvas = document.getElementById("crtMask");
    var glowCanvas = document.getElementById("crtGlow");
    if (!maskCanvas || !glowCanvas) return;

    var VERT =
      "attribute vec2 p;\n" +
      "void main(){ gl_Position = vec4(p, 0.0, 1.0); }";

    var PRECISION =
      "#ifdef GL_FRAGMENT_PRECISION_HIGH\n" +
      "precision highp float;\n" +
      "#else\n" +
      "precision mediump float;\n" +
      "#endif\n";

    /* 减光：荫罩 RGB 子像素、扫描线、暗角恒常；
       噪点与亮度抖动只在停表时介入（stat 0→1）。 */
    var FRAG_MASK =
      PRECISION +
      "uniform vec2 res;\n" +
      "uniform float t;\n" +
      "uniform float dpr;\n" +
      "uniform float stat;\n" +
      "float hash(vec2 p){ return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(41.3, 289.1))) * 43758.5453); }\n" +
      "float vnoise(vec2 p){\n" +
      "  vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p);\n" +
      "  vec2 u = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);\n" +
      "  return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), u.x),\n" +
      "             mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), u.x), u.y);\n" +
      "}\n" +
      "void main(){\n" +
      "  vec2 frag = gl_FragCoord.xy;\n" +
      "  vec2 uv = frag / res;\n" +
      "  vec2 c = uv - 0.5;\n" +
      "  float aspect = res.x / max(res.y, 1.0);\n" +
      "  float period = max(2.0, 2.6 * dpr);\n" +
      "  float sl = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(frag.y * 6.2831853 / period);\n" +
      "  vec3 att = vec3(mix(0.74, 1.0, sl));\n" +
      "  float g = max(1.0, dpr);\n" +
      "  float ph = mod(frag.x, 3.0 * g);\n" +
      "  vec3 sub;\n" +
      "  if (ph < g) sub = vec3(1.0, 0.55, 0.55);\n" +
      "  else if (ph < 2.0 * g) sub = vec3(0.55, 1.0, 0.55);\n" +
      "  else sub = vec3(0.55, 0.55, 1.0);\n" +
      "  att *= mix(vec3(1.0), sub, 0.28);\n" +
      "  float r = length(vec2(c.x * aspect * 0.68, c.y));\n" +
      "  float vig = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.4, 1.06, r);\n" +
      "  att *= mix(0.56, 1.0, vig);\n" +
      "  float n = vnoise(frag * 0.75 + vec2(t * 26.0, -t * 17.0));\n" +
      "  att *= 1.0 - stat * 0.12 * n;\n" +
      "  att *= 1.0 - stat * 0.05 * (0.5 + 0.5 * sin(t * 43.0));\n" +
      "  att *= mix(1.0, 0.9 + 0.08 * n, stat);\n" +
      "  gl_FragColor = vec4(clamp(att, 0.0, 1.0), 1.0);\n" +
      "}";

    /* 加光：中央荧光晕恒常；滚屏亮带与静电亮点只在停表时出现 */
    var FRAG_GLOW =
      PRECISION +
      "uniform vec2 res;\n" +
      "uniform float t;\n" +
      "uniform float stat;\n" +
      "float hash(vec2 p){ return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(41.3, 289.1))) * 43758.5453); }\n" +
      "void main(){\n" +
      "  vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / res;\n" +
      "  vec2 c = uv - 0.5;\n" +
      "  float aspect = res.x / max(res.y, 1.0);\n" +
      "  float r = length(vec2(c.x * aspect, c.y));\n" +
      "  vec3 glow = vec3(0.0);\n" +
      "  glow += vec3(0.42, 0.62, 1.0) * exp(-r * r * 5.0) * 0.04;\n" +
      "  float rollY = fract(0.72 - t * 0.05);\n" +
      "  float d = (uv.y - rollY) / 0.04;\n" +
      "  glow += vec3(0.55, 0.72, 1.0) * exp(-d * d) * 0.1 * stat;\n" +
      "  float sp = step(0.9986, hash(gl_FragCoord.xy + vec2(floor(t * 24.0))));\n" +
      "  glow += vec3(0.75, 0.85, 1.0) * sp * 0.14 * stat;\n" +
      "  gl_FragColor = vec4(glow, 1.0);\n" +
      "}";

    function contextOf(canvas) {
      var opts = {
        alpha: true,
        antialias: false,
        depth: false,
        stencil: false,
        powerPreference: "high-performance"
      };
      var gl = null;
      try { gl = canvas.getContext("webgl2", opts); } catch (e) { gl = null; }
      if (!gl) {
        try { gl = canvas.getContext("webgl", opts); } catch (e2) { gl = null; }
      }
      return gl;
    }

    function compile(gl, type, src) {
      var s = gl.createShader(type);
      gl.shaderSource(s, src);
      gl.compileShader(s);
      if (!gl.getShaderParameter(s, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
        console.error("[CRT] shader compile failed:", gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));
        return null;
      }
      return s;
    }

    function buildPass(canvas, fragSrc) {
      var gl = contextOf(canvas);
      if (!gl) return null;
      var vs = compile(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERT);
      var fs = compile(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragSrc);
      if (!vs || !fs) return null;
      var prog = gl.createProgram();
      gl.attachShader(prog, vs);
      gl.attachShader(prog, fs);
      gl.linkProgram(prog);
      if (!gl.getProgramParameter(prog, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
        console.error("[CRT] program link failed:", gl.getProgramInfoLog(prog));
        return null;
      }
      gl.useProgram(prog);
      var buf = gl.createBuffer();
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buf);
      gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1, -1, 3, -1, -1, 3]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
      var loc = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, "p");
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(loc);
      gl.vertexAttribPointer(loc, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
      return {
        gl: gl,
        canvas: canvas,
        uRes: gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "res"),
        uT: gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "t"),
        uDpr: gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "dpr"),
        uStat: gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "stat"),
        dpr: 1
      };
    }

    var mask = buildPass(maskCanvas, FRAG_MASK);
    var glow = buildPass(glowCanvas, FRAG_GLOW);
    if (!mask) maskCanvas.style.display = "none";
    if (!glow) glowCanvas.style.display = "none";
    if (!mask && !glow) {
      document.body.classList.add("no-gl");
      return;
    }

    var mq = window.matchMedia
      ? window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)")
      : null;
    function animated() { return !(mq && mq.matches); }

    function resizePass(pass) {
      var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      var w = Math.max(1, Math.round(pass.canvas.clientWidth * dpr));
      var h = Math.max(1, Math.round(pass.canvas.clientHeight * dpr));
      if (w !== pass.canvas.width || h !== pass.canvas.height) {
        pass.canvas.width = w;
        pass.canvas.height = h;
      }
      pass.dpr = dpr;
      pass.gl.viewport(0, 0, pass.canvas.width, pass.canvas.height);
    }

    function drawPass(pass, t, stat) {
      resizePass(pass);
      var gl = pass.gl;
      gl.uniform2f(pass.uRes, pass.canvas.width, pass.canvas.height);
      gl.uniform1f(pass.uT, t);
      gl.uniform1f(pass.uDpr, pass.dpr);
      gl.uniform1f(pass.uStat, stat);
      gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);
    }

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
      if (mask) resizePass(mask);
      if (glow) resizePass(glow);
    });

    var start = performance.now();
    var checked = false;
    var stat = 0;

    function tick(now) {
      var t = (now - start) / 1000;
      var paused = document.body.classList.contains("paused");
      // 走时中完全不绘制 CRT 着色，画面保持干净；停表后整层淡入，
      // 噪点/抖动再做短暂过渡；开启"减少动态效果"时保持静止。
      var target = animated() && paused ? 1.0 : 0.0;
      stat += (target - stat) * 0.12;
      if (Math.abs(target - stat) < 0.001) stat = target;

      if (paused) {
        if (mask) drawPass(mask, t, stat);
        if (glow) drawPass(glow, t, stat);
      }

      if (!checked && paused && (mask || glow)) {
        checked = true;
        var err = mask ? mask.gl.getError() : glow.gl.getError();
        if (err) console.error("[CRT] GL error after first frame:", err);
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    // 自动化检查钩子（不影响页面行为）
    window.__crt = {
      mask: !!mask,
      glow: !!glow,
      reducedMotion: !animated()
    };
  })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

# Lab 2 Part 2

## Prep 

1. Pick up remaining parts for kit on Wednesday lab class. Check the updated [parts list inventory](partslist.md) and let the TA know if there is any part missing.

2. Look at and give feedback on the Part E. for at least 3 other people in the class and get 3 people to comment on your Part E!)
**Put the feedback for your ideas here.**

## Update your Lab Hub

[Update your Lab Hub](pull_updates/README.md) to get the latest content and requirements for Part 2.

## Modify the barebones clock to make it your own

Start small, pick just one element of your overall idea, just to show you have a handle on the code and components.

\*\*\***Put a copy of your code in your Lab 2 Github repo.**\*\*\*

## Make a short video of your modified barebones PiClock

\*\*\***Take a video of your barely modified PiClock.**\*\*\*

After you edit and work on the scripts for Lab 2, the files should be upload back to your own GitHub repo! You can push to your personal github repo by adding the files here, commiting and pushing.

```
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ git add .
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ git commit -m 'your commit message here'
(venv) pi@raspberrypi:~/Interactive-Lab-Hub/Lab 2 $ git push
```

After that, Git will ask you to login to your GitHub account to push the updates online, you will be asked to provide your GitHub user name and password. Remember to use the "Personal Access Tokens" you set up in Part A as the password instead of your account one! Go on your GitHub repo with your laptop, you should be able to see the updated files from your Pi!

## Now, make your own PiClock

Do take advantage of having done the previous iteration to refine and simplify your design.

** Insert any updates ideas, sketches, [Verplank diagrams](https://ccrma.stanford.edu/courses/250a-fall-2004/IDSketchbok.pdf))!, storyboards for your ideas **


\*\*\***Put a copy of your code in your Lab 2 Github repo.**\*\*\*

\*\*\***Take a video of your PiClock.**\*\*\*


As always, make sure you document contributions and ideas from others (and AI) explicitly in your writeup.

You are permitted (but not required) to work in groups and share a turn in; you are expected to make equal contribution on any group work you do, and N people's group project should look like N times the work of a single person's lab.  Make sure the page for the group turn in is linked to your personal Interactive Lab Hub page.
